- **metabolite-structure-dereplication-against-hmdb**: Use when a COSMIC/SIRIUS annotation table of structure candidates must be reduced to high-confidence novel structures absent from HMDB or spectral libraries, or when auditing per-cohort annotation counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-dereplication-against-hmdb))
- **metabolite-structure-format-conversion**: Use when when importing candidate metabolite structures from public chemical databases (PubChem, ChEBI, etc.) for use in MAGMa-based annotation workflows, or when integrating external structure datasets that may use divergent molecular representation formats or contain non-standard chemical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-format-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when CypBoM has flagged reactive bonds (〈X, Y〉 pairs with p ≥ τ = 1/(β+1)) for a substrate–CYP450 isoform pair and you must convert those BoM predictions into concrete metabolite structures with per-metabolite scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))
- **metabolite-structure-generation**: Use when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to predict its metabolic fate in a specific biological context (mammalian phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-generation))
- **metabolite-structure-matching-against-reference-sets**: Use when you must reproduce or compare precision/recall/Jaccard performance of CYP450 metabolite-prediction tools against a curated true-metabolite reference set like EBoMD2 or the BioTransformer hold-out dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-structure-matching-against-reference-sets))
- **metabolite-structure-prediction**: Use when you have a parent compound (or set of compounds) in SMILES, MOL, or SDF format and need to predict plausible metabolite structures and pathways in a specific biological context (mammalian Phase I/II metabolism, human gut microbiota, or soil/aquatic microbial degradation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-structure-prediction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-subnetwork-extraction**: Use when analyzing high-dimensional longitudinal metabolomics concentration tables (e.g., 1H-NMR profiles of sequential cell populations) and you need to identify which metabolites and covariance relationships define a specific biological transition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-subnetwork-extraction))
- **metabolite-superclass-stratified-sampling**: Use when benchmarking in silico spectral prediction or identification tools across metabolomics domains (exposomics, foodomics, human metabolomics) and you need unbiased, domain-representative test sets drawn from a larger spectral database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-superclass-stratified-sampling))
- **metabolite-synonym-reconciliation**: Use when two metabolite annotation engines (e.g. SpaMTP and METASPACE/pySM) annotate overlapping m/z peaks from the same spatial metabolomics dataset and synonym naming differences must be reconciled before computing per-database match percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-synonym-reconciliation))
- **metabolite-synonym-resolution**: Use when a metabolomics meta-analysis input table mixes identifier types (compound synonyms, InChIKeys, SMILES) so that the same compound appears under multiple labels and would otherwise inflate unique-ID counts and deflate vote counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-synonym-resolution))
- **metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation**: Use when you have multiple tandem MS/MS libraries in different formats (msp, mgf) from different providers (NIST, RIKEN, MoNA, GNPS) with incomplete or inconsistent structural annotations (missing SMILES or molecular formula fields) and need to combine them into unified, polarity-specific msp files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-tandem-ms-library-curation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-target-definition**: Use when building a targeted LC-MS assay in TarMet and you must define target metabolite m/z windows from a molecular formula/adduct, or derive isotopologue m/z series for a stable-isotope tracer with a chosen isotope and max isotopologues N. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-target-definition))
- **metabolite-target-list-formatting**: Use when you have a raw LC–MS compound metadata file (xlsx or csv) with heterogeneous column names and column order, and you need to prepare it for targeted peak detection in TARDIS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-target-list-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup**: Use when when you have MS/MS-annotated features from a natural extract (via SIRIUS, CANOPUS, or ISDB) and need to compute the Literature Component or Class Component of a priority rank—i. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-taxonomy-database-lookup) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-gene-mapping**: Use when you have metabolomic data (e.g., from LC-MS or GC-MS comparing patient to controls) showing differential abundant metabolites (DAMs), candidate genes from exome sequencing or variant calling, and access to a protein–protein or gene–gene interaction network (e.g., STRING). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-gene-mapping) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-to-pathway-mapping**: Use when you have a peak intensity matrix (samples × metabolites) with assigned metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG or ChEBI compound ID), and you need to link those identities to known metabolic pathways, molecular families, or mass2motifs before computing pathway activity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-to-pathway-mapping))
- **metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping**: Use when comparing a metabolite/Byproduct-predicting tool against SoM-only predictors on a common benchmark, so predicted products must be converted to atom-level SoM labels (e.g., TP/(TP+FN+FP) Jaccard over CYP450 isoforms). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-to-som-annotation-mapping))
- **metabolite-trait-gwas-correction**: Use when running mGWAS of a pan-genome orthogene matrix against a large numeric phenotypic dataset (e.g. metabolomics traits per strain) and you must control false positives with trait-aware correction before nominating candidate genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metabolite-trait-gwas-correction))
- **metabolite-trend-classification**: Use when when you have compiled p-values and fold-changes from multiple metabolomics studies and need to assign a single trend label (up/down/none) to each metabolite for meta-analysis or cross-study comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification))
- **metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation**: Use when input data consist only of compound identifiers and trend/behaviour labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend) per study — i.e. studies that do not disclose p-values, fold-changes, or standard deviations — and a qualitative per-compound meta-analysis is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-trend-classification-updown-regulation))
- **metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing**: Use when a processed LC-MS feature table (samples x metabolite features with group labels) must be tested feature-by-feature for differential abundance between two classes, producing fold change, p-value, and adjusted p-value per feature for volcano plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-univariate-statistical-testing))
- **metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization**: Use when you have a metabolomics matrix and an lmm2met fit object and need to visually validate that linear mixed-effects modelling reduced per-metabolite variance, as in Figure S-1/Fig. 3a. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-variance-boxplot-visualization))
- **metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis**: Use when you have metabolomic results from multiple studies with only compound identifiers, directional trend labels (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend), and bibliographic references — and you want to quantify agreement on metabolite behavior direction across studies without requiring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-vote-counting-qualitative-analysis))
- **metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling**: Use when preprocessing urine metabolite intensity tables (e.g. synthetic_metabolomics.csv) for downstream integration, where metabolites differ in variance and must be scaled before MOFA or similarity network analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-wise-pareto-scaling))
- **metabolites-assignment-table-construction**: Use when the goal is to convert a cohort of raw 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz) into an automated, per-sample quantitative table for a predefined panel of ~22 abundant metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolites-assignment-table-construction))
- **metabolization-review**: Use this skill when a user asks whether a repeated capability, workflow, tool, habit, or agent behavior has become substrate through settlement, repetition, time, stability, and lower attention burden. ([GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent](https://github.com/GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolization-review))
- **metabolizing-knowledge**: Use when asked whether something delivered earlier still holds, when closing a milestone or running a periodic sweep over what was already marked verified, or when the set of live claims has grown too large to read and dead entries need retiring. DO NOT invoke to add, audit, or retire a decision record — route that to ledger:curating-decision-records. DO NOT invoke to propose removing code — route that to ledger:proving-code-is-dead. ([qbs784/ledger](https://github.com/qbs784/ledger/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolizing-knowledge))
- **metabologenomic-correlation-scoring**: Use when a strain collection has both genome-mined BGCs (with iPRESTO sub-cluster motifs or SCCs) and MS/MS data with Mass2Motifs, and the goal is to link molecular substructures to their biosynthetic sub-clusters via co-occurrence correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabologenomic-correlation-scoring))
- **metabologenomic-database-construction**: Use when you have genome FASTA or annotated genome files (antiSMASH .gbk, BOA .annotated.txt) and wish to discover ribosomally synthesized and post-translationally modified peptides (RiPPs) by integrating genomic and mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabologenomic-database-construction) · 3 copies)
- **metabolome-genome-link-tabulation**: Use when you have downloaded the PoDP Zenodo JSON deposit and need to reproduce or audit the published dataset statistics (Box 1) — e.g. validating that 1,306 metagenomes, 1,268 genomes, and 42 MAGs are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-genome-link-tabulation))
- **metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction**: Use when a labeled metabolomics feature table (samples × metabolite intensities with phenotype metadata, e.g. age, sex, region) is available and the goal is an automated, metabolome-informed predictive classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-informed-phenotype-prediction))
- **metabolome-module-feature-aggregation**: Use when you have MiMeNet biclustered microbe/metabolite modules and WGCNA modules for paired microbiome-metabolome data (e.g. IBD PRISM) and want to determine if module-level features match original features for predicting disease labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-module-feature-aggregation))
- **metabolome-wide-association-analysis**: Use when a SummarizedExperiment of metabolic feature intensities (e.g. plasma 1H NMR) plus clinical metadata (phenotype + confounders) must be screened feature-by-feature for phenotype associations with multiple-testing control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolome-wide-association-analysis))
- **metabolomic-biomarker-pathway-association**: Use when after marker identification (via fold-change, PLS-DA, t-test, or other feature selection methods) has produced a ranked list of metabolic features. - **metabolomic-data-format-standardization**: Use when you have raw peak table data from liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry (LC-MS) or related metabolomic instruments, generated by one of 12 supported software tools (or already in NOREVA's standardized format), and need to prepare it for preprocessing method evaluation or biomarker. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-format-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration**: Use when immediately after integrating multiple metabolomic datasets from different analytical experiments into a unified feature-by-sample matrix. Batch effects manifest as systematic unwanted variations correlated with analytical batch rather than biological sample groups; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-normalization-post-integration))
- **metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization**: Use when you have multi-class or time-course metabolomic peak tables (with or without quality control samples and/or internal standards) and need to select the preprocessing workflow from hundreds of candidate combinations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-preprocessing-optimization))
- **metabolomic-data-quality-assessment**: Use when when you have raw metabolomics data with missing values in metabolite columns and need to prepare the dataset for downstream statistical analysis (linear mixed models, ANOVA, dimensionality reduction, or prediction models). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-quality-assessment))
- **metabolomic-data-structure-formatting**: Use when after peak detection in MZmine2 has produced an MGF file (containing MS1 and MS2 spectra) and a feature abundance table (CSV or BIOM), but before running q2-qemistree tree construction or any QIIME 2-based metabolomic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-structure-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-data-subsetting-by-phenotype**: Use when you have a preprocessed MultiAssayExperiment with metabolite measurements across multiple phenotypic or disease subgroups, and you want to isolate one or more groups for comparison—for example, to compare AKT1-high versus MYC-high prostate tumor samples before applying diff_test for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-data-subsetting-by-phenotype))
- **metabolomic-data-transformation-and-normalization**: Use when a metabolomic peak table has passed filtering/imputation (S1) and QCS correction (S2) and must be nonlinearly transformed (e.g., LOG) and normalized (e.g., VAS-LIN) before performance assessment in NOREVA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-data-transformation-and-normalization))
- **metabolomic-dataset-preprocessing-and-normalization**: Use when you have raw or minimally processed FT-ICR MS peak tables in Formularity . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-dataset-preprocessing-and-normalization))
- **metabolomic-edge-list-import-processing**: Use when you have edge-list files of putative biochemical transformations from FT-ICR MS peak mass differences and need a Cytoscape network plus network statistics tables and plots. - **metabolomic-enrichment-score-scaling**: Use when you have per-patient DAM lists (absolute z-score &gt;= 2) from XCMS untargeted LC-MS output and need continuous [0,1] per-gene seed labels for metPropagate label propagation over STRING. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-enrichment-score-scaling))
- **metabolomic-feature-alignment**: Use when you have two or more CSV feature tables from independent metabolomic experiments (each with RT, m/z, intensity, isotope, and adduct columns), and you need to merge them into a single aligned feature matrix for downstream batch effect removal, marker identification, or pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-alignment) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-count-comparison**: Use when evaluating a hybrid DDA/DIA acquisition strategy against conventional DDA and DIA(SWATH) runs and you need reproducible feature/MS2-count tables with recomputed relative gains. - **metabolomic-feature-extraction-from-mzml**: Use when you have centroid mzML files from LC-MS acquisitions and need to detect, group, and quantify metabolomic features for a PCPFM experiment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-extraction-from-mzml) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-filtering-threshold-application**: Use when after running the Marr() function on preprocessed metabolomic data (with missing value imputation and normalization already complete) when you need to distinguish reproducible from non-reproducible features and sample pairs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-filtering-threshold-application))
- **metabolomic-feature-imputation**: Use when when a preprocessed metabolomic feature table (e.g., MS-Dial output) retains features and samples that passed filtering for missingness thresholds and m/z validity, but still contain scattered missing values (NA). This skill is appropriate after sample-level filtering (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-imputation))
- **metabolomic-feature-matching**: Use when you have two LC-MS feature tables (each with m/z, retention time, and intensity columns) from separate metabolomic experiments or replicates, and you need to establish which features in dataset A correspond to which features in dataset B to enable comparative or longitudinal analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-matching) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-recovery-quantification**: Use when comparing LC-MS acquisition workflows (DDA vs DIA vs hybrid DaDIA) and you need to measure how many DDA-derived metabolic features are recovered in the combined data and assess their peak-shape reliability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-feature-recovery-quantification))
- **metabolomic-feature-retention-statistics**: Use when after applying the CV_ratio() filtering function to a normalized metabolomic feature matrix (e.g., Urine_RP_NEG_norm.txt) in margheRita, generate retention statistics to report how many features passed the threshold (CV ratio &gt; 1.0) and characterize the distribution of retained CV ratios. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomic-feature-retention-statistics) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomic-feature-selection-by-metadata**: Use when a quality-controlled metabolomic feature table plus sample metadata (e.g., age group, sex, brain region) is available and the goal is metadata-driven feature selection and phenotype prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-feature-selection-by-metadata))
- **metabolomic-feature-significance-filtering**: Use when you have a paired-design LC-MS metabolomic feature table (e.g. from XCMS centWave on DDA or DaDIA data) and need to identify statistically significant metabolic features for downstream workflow comparison or metabolite annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomic-feature-significance-filtering))

- **metabolomics-annotation-counting**: Use when comparing annotation coverage of competing open spectral libraries on the same MS2 feature table — e.g. asking how many extra annotations a new library adds over existing ones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-correction-evaluation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-correction-pca-visualization))
- **metabolomics-batch-diagnostics-pca-plotting**: Use when raw targeted or untargeted LC-MS metabolomics data must be checked for analytical batch effects before choosing a correction model, e.g. samples appear grouped by run order. - **metabolomics-batch-effect-adjustment**: Use when you have a log-transformed metabolomics featuredata matrix with missing values handled (via knn or replacement) and you observe systematic variation across batches, quality control samples, or sample order that is NOT of biological interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-adjustment))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-correction**: Use when a log2-transformed LC–MS metabolomics matrix (.csv, samples in rows, batch labels in column 1) shows batch-dependent clustering (e.g., PCA grouping by batch order) and you need to compare batch-correction models quantitatively. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-correction))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-correction**: Use when you have log-transformed, imputed metabolomics data from multiple experimental batches (rawImpute assay) that shows visible batch effects in PCA plots or signal drift across runs, and your study design includes intra-batch replicates (technical replicates within a run) and inter-batch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-correction))
- **metabolomics-batch-effect-removal**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS peak intensity matrix (e.g., XCMS/CAMERA output) with a per-sample injection order vector, but batch labels are missing or there is only one batch, and batch/drift effects must be removed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-batch-effect-removal))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-comparison**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking peak-detection/quality-filtering outputs from competing LC-MS metabolomics tools on the same public dataset, and when validating that repeated executions produce identical counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-comparison--557643d66e4f584e))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-comparison**: Use when comparing LC-MS metabolomics feature-extraction/annotation software head-to-head on the same raw or pre-processed data with pre-optimized parameters, e.g. urine RP(+) feature counts across JPA vs MZmine 2, XCMS, MS-DIAL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-comparison--d9eb957b361c81b2))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-count-reproduction**: Use when validating or reproducing quantitative claims from a metabolomics methods paper — e.g., '105 of 177 compounds yielded CSA spectra, 25,351 total spectra' — using the authors' own public dataset and toolchain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-count-reproduction))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-dataset-processing**: Use when benchmarking an untargeted LC-MS/MS processing pipeline against published feature lists from public MetaboLights datasets, to quantify what fraction of published features (per ionization mode) your workflow recovers and whether quantification agrees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-dataset-processing))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-metric-computation**: Reference for quantifying annotation quality when a metabolomics annotator proposes KEGG compounds per LC-MS peak and a ground-truth compound set per assay exists; needed to reproduce per-assay TP/FP/FN tables, sensitivity (e.g. 0.63 vs xMSannotator 0.55), F1 (up to 0.49 for mWISE vs 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-metric-computation))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a paper reports a quantitative mapping or annotation rate for a metabolomics identifier-resolution tool on a named published dataset and you need to independently verify that number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--822617e4f552496c))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when the goal is to reproduce or validate a published metabolomics analysis — such as matching the SMART 2.0 narcotics or breast-cancer demo outputs, or replicating LLM peak-identification results on 12 narcotics m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--dc7c32b020a86efe))
- **metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when a task requires validating or reproducing published feature-alignment results for LC-HRMS metabolomics data, especially simulated ion-species swap tests comparing a reference feature list against a target ion list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-benchmark-reproduction--009d292b17f0496e))
- **metabolomics-biomarker-hypothesis-testing**: Use when a two-group metabolomics comparison requires simultaneous multiple-hypothesis testing across all metabolite features with calibrated fdr2d cutoff rejection counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-biomarker-hypothesis-testing))
- **metabolomics-blank-feature-filtering**: Use when an untargeted metabolomics feature-intensity table contains blank samples alongside experimental samples and features must be flagged/removed based on blank-derived limits of detection before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-blank-feature-filtering))
- **metabolomics-blank-masking**: Use when an Asari-derived feature table contains features plausibly attributable to blanks/contaminants and a sample metadata CSV annotates each sample's sample_type (e.g. 'Blank' vs 'Unknown'). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-blank-masking))
- **metabolomics-ce-ms-router**: Use for any metabolomics — CE-MS how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. Searches 113 evidence-grounded metabolomics — CE-MS skills distilled from method papers and their code, then reads the one that fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/packs/metabolomics/ce-ms/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-chemical-mixture-generation-from-hmdb**: Use when you need to create realistic, diverse chemical populations for simulating LC-MS/MS acquisition strategies in a virtual environment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-chemical-mixture-generation-from-hmdb) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-chromatogram-alignment**: Use when multiple LC-HRMS measurement sequences from untargeted metabolomics experiments exhibit m/z and/or retention time shifts and need to be matched against a reference feature list to build a combined peak-area data matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-chromatogram-alignment))
- **metabolomics-chromatogram-object-manipulation**: Use when two or more separately imported/preprocessed 2D chromatogram objects (e.g., NetCDF-derived 2D-TICs from MetaboLights studies) must be merged into one R structure before multivariate analysis such as MPCA via m_prcomp. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-chromatogram-object-manipulation))
- **metabolomics-class-prediction-evaluation**: Use when you need to benchmark whether a metabolomics data-processing strategy (e.g., LMM fitting vs. original data vs. multilevel PLS-DA) improves downstream class-prediction performance on labeled samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-class-prediction-evaluation))
- **metabolomics-classification-modeling**: Use when a metabolomics sample-by-metabolite matrix with phenotype labels needs pathway-level feature selection and classification modeling at the individual sample level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-classification-modeling))
- **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature table with class labels needs supervised classification models whose accuracy must be measured on held-out test samples rather than on the full data set. - **metabolomics-classifier-training**: Use when you have a preprocessed metabolomics feature matrix (expression matrix with metabolite abundances as columns and samples as rows) with corresponding binary or multi-class sample labels, and you need to train and compare classifier performance to select the -performing model for disease. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-classifier-training) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-collection-router**: Use when an agent needs to find and apply a computational-metabolomics / LC-MS-MS skill from this collection, and optionally ground it against the source paper via Perspicacité before acting. - **metabolomics-combined-table-filtering**: Use when a scored metabCombiner combined table contains many conflicting or low-scoring feature pair alignments (e.g. 14024 FPAs) and the goal is a reduced final table of intersecting features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-combined-table-filtering))
- **metabolomics-compound-confidence-classification**: Use when a batch-corrected targeted metabolomics peak-integrated table must be screened per compound to decide which metabolites are reliable enough to report, flagging those with poor QC reproducibility or high blank background before downstream statistics. - **metabolomics-compound-direction-classification**: Use when contributing studies report only aggregate statistics (p-value, fold-change, sample size) per compound and you need a per-compound regulation direction label from the meta-analyzed log2 fold-change. - **metabolomics-concentration-data-transformation**: Use when a post-normalization metabolite concentration table (.csv) is right-skewed or spans a wide dynamic range and an element-wise Log10, Log2, square root, or cube root transform is needed before scaling and PCA-based data-treatment assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-concentration-data-transformation))
- **metabolomics-condition-wise-count-aggregation**: Use when an XCMS peak table from stable-isotope (13C) labeling experiments has been processed with PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder and you need to count how many features show isotopic-distribution shifts per condition. - **metabolomics-confounder-adjustment**: Use when a raw metabolomics peak-intensity table (e.g., TEDDY Metabolomics Workbench ST001386) will feed network estimation or differential analysis, and sample covariates like age and sex must be removed from metabolite abundances first. - **metabolomics-contrast-group-comparison**: Use when you have a preprocessed LC-MS/MS feature quantification table with k-medoids cluster assignments from spectral similarity grouping and need feature-set level differential testing between two treatment groups. - **metabolomics-correlation-network-construction**: Use when building metabolite-metabolite association networks from peak-intensity tables, or when explaining why marginal-correlation networks are denser than partial-correlation networks on the same data. - **metabolomics-count-tabulation**: Use when two or more annotation strategies have already assigned results to the same MS/MS peak list (e.g., motif probability memberships and Mass Bank/NIST library hits) and you need comparable coverage counts, per-mode percentages, and a yield ratio. - **metabolomics-cross-dataset-correspondence**: Use when two separately peak-picked LC–MS metabolomics feature lists (e.g. XCMS output tables with RT, m/z, FI medians) need to be matched across batches, studies, or platforms without shared internal standards. - **metabolomics-cross-study-meta-analysis**: Use when many GC/MS (or similar) metabolomics studies must be compared across broad biological categories — e.g., 'human digestive tract' vs 'bacteria' — and study metadata are heterogeneous, unstructured, or ontology-definable. - **metabolomics-curation**: Comprehensive toolkit for curating untargeted metabolomics features from LC-MS data. Supports Excel/CSV inputs with ~100-300 features and generates interactive HTML reports following FGCZ standards. - **metabolomics-data-downstream-analysis**: Use when you have deposited spatio-molecular matrices (e.g., MORPHnMOL.csv from SpaceM analysis) and need to reproduce or extend the data transformations, feature extractions, and figure generation reported in a metabolomics manuscript. - **metabolomics-data-exclusion-criteria-validation**: Use when you have received or published a claim about the number of metabolites excluded by a missingness-based quality filter (e.g., 'cutoff=0. - **metabolomics-data-filtering**: Use when putative microbial or taxon-linked MS/MS spectra (.mgf, e.g. from MZmine feature detection) need to be cross-validated against an independent dataset with a metadata contrast such as antibiotic-treated vs untreated samples. - **metabolomics-data-format-handling**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data in mzML or equivalent binary format from a public repository (MetaboLights, MassIVE) or instrument vendor output, and need to ingest it into MetaboAnalystR 4.0 for unified LC-MS1 feature detection and MS/MS spectra processing. - **metabolomics-data-format-validation**: Use when you have a tab-delimited metabolomics data file (raw measurement output from xcms, Sciex OS, or similar acquisition pipelines) and need to load it into mzQuality before building a SummarizedExperiment. - **metabolomics-data-formatting**: Use when after running feature clustering (Gravity) or drift correction (Blueshift) on LCMS data, when you have a processed feature table and need to standardize its structure, validate metadata completeness, enforce missing-value thresholds, and generate a QC report documenting pass/fail status. - **metabolomics-data-import-and-parsing**: Use when when you have metabolomics comparison results from one or more studies in tabular format (spreadsheet or text file) with columns for compound name/identifier, statistical p-value, relative fold-change (including negative values indicating down-regulation), study sample size (N), and. - **metabolomics-data-ingestion**: Use when two CSVs — a target/metadata file (sample names, group labels, optional covariates) and a features file — must be merged into an analysis-ready MSnSet object for metabolomics/proteomics analysis. - **metabolomics-data-input-validation**: Use when when importing a tab-delimited or Sciex OS text export metabolomics dataset into mzQuality, before building the SummarizedExperiment object. - **metabolomics-data-integration**: Use when two standardized omics views — e.g. creatinine-normalized urine metabolomics intensities and top-10%-variable autosomal buccal DNA methylation probes — need to be integrated into shared latent factors without supervision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-loading-and-formatting))
- **metabolomics-data-loading-into-containers**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data (e.g., in standard bioinformatics formats supported by maplet) and need to initialize an analytical pipeline that will integrate multiple processing and analysis steps. - **metabolomics-data-matrix-handling**: Use when when you have raw metabolomics peak intensities or concentrations and accompanying sample metadata (batch, run order, sample type, factors of interest) and need to organize them into the featuredata, sampledata, and metabolitedata dataframes that NormalizeMets functions expect. - **metabolomics-data-matrix-manipulation**: Use when you need synthetic MNAR (left-censored) missingness in a complete targeted/un-targeted metabolomics matrix to benchmark imputation methods such as GSimp, QRILC, HM, or kNN-TN. - **metabolomics-data-normalization**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data (samples × metabolic features matrix) collected across multiple analytical batches or instrument runs, and you observe evidence of technical heterogeneity—such as systematic shifts in metabolite intensities between batches, missing or zero values, or visual. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-quality-control))
- **metabolomics-data-quality-metrics**: Use when you have a Sciex Multiquant (≥v3.0.3) txt export containing QCpool sample measurements at multiple timepoints within a sequence, and you need to flag compounds with high technical variability or signal degradation before proceeding to statistical analysis or interpretation of. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-quality-metrics) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-representation**: Use when you have a raw MGF file containing fragmented LC-MS-MS metabolomics spectra and want to apply Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) to discover hidden topics (molecular families, biochemical patterns) across your sample set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-representation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-data-scaling**: Use when a normalized, transformed metabolomics table (e.g., post Log10/Log2/square-root/cube-root transformation) still spans a large dynamic range across metabolites and needs variance-scaled columns before statistical analysis or PCA-based data treatment evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-scaling))
- **metabolomics-data-simulation-from-real-datasets**: Use when the goal is to benchmark imputation algorithms on real metabolomics data by injecting a known missingness pattern (10% MNAR below the 10%-quantile LOD, 5% MAR) into complete data and scoring downstream metabolite-list concordance (MLCI). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-simulation-from-real-datasets))
- **metabolomics-data-standardization**: Use when you have raw 1D NMR spectral data (urine, worm, or other biological samples) that needs to be converted into peak tables for metabolite identification and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-standardization))
- **metabolomics-data-standardization**: Use when raw NMR metabolomics files acquired from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench need conversion into an analysis-ready format for statistical analysis, or when reproducing a published analysis from a repository study id. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-standardization))
- **metabolomics-data-structure-handling**: Use when when beginning an LC-MS/MS metabolomics analysis pipeline and you have preprocessed xcms result objects (XcmsExperiment or legacy xcmsSet) that need to be loaded into memory, validated for integrity, and prepared for feature grouping (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolomics-data-structure-handling))
- **metabolomics-data-structure-handling**: Use when you have metabolomics data already formatted as a SummarizedExperiment object containing multiple assays (raw counts, log-transformed, imputed, or normalized versions) and need to access specific assay layers alongside batch annotation metadata to perform comparisons (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-structure-handling))
- **metabolomics-data-subsetting**: Use when you need to assess how sample size affects the estimated correlation structure of high-dimensional metabolite abundances, or to build simulated null scenarios for validating multiple-testing corrections like Meff0 = alpha / alpha_f0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-subsetting))
- **metabolomics-data-summarization**: Use when TarMet tracer quantification has already produced per-sample isotopologue peak areas (e.g. for 13C6-glucose-traced glycolysis metabolites such as G6P, F6P, FBP, DHAP, Pyr, Lac) and you need a normalized fractional-labelling summary to verify pathway labelling patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-summarization))
- **metabolomics-data-table-export**: Use when PuInc_seeker (ULtag='CELL_Glc12', Ltag='CELL_Glc13') and basepeak_finder (UL.atomM=12.0, L.atomM=13.003355, ppm.s=6.5, Basepeak.minInt=2000) results exist and a flat CSV of labeled features is the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-data-table-export))
- **metabolomics-data-validation**: Use when you have raw or semi-processed m/z peak lists (positive and negative ion mode) and a sample metadata table, and you need to confirm they meet MetaboShiny's structural and semantic requirements before loading them into the normalization pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-data-validation))
- **metabolomics-database-capability-assessment**: Use when evaluating whether metabolomics data repositories support programmatic sample-level meta-analysis, e.g. comparing Metabolomics Workbench, ReDU and MetaboLights design choices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-database-capability-assessment))
- **metabolomics-database-matching**: Use when geoRge's PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder have already produced labeled-feature base peaks on an XCMS feature table and the goal is to assign putative metabolite identities from a database such as ExampleDatabase.csv. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-database-matching))
- **metabolomics-database-search-and-formula-matching**: Use when after you have detected LC-MS features, grouped them into empirical compounds via isotope and adduct clustering (using khipu), and have accurate m/z and retention time values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-database-search-and-formula-matching) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-dataset-acquisition-metabolights**: Use when a study requires an external, publicly archived LC-MS metabolomics cohort (raw mzML files with disease labels) to train or benchmark a classifier, and the dataset of interest has a MetaboLights accession ID. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-acquisition-metabolights))
- **metabolomics-dataset-curation**: Use when curating large batches of public METASPACE imaging MS datasets for downstream model training or evaluation, especially when non-centroided, low-annotation, or oversaturated datasets must be excluded systematically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-curation))
- **metabolomics-dataset-handling-massive**: Use when you are beginning a non-targeted metabolomics analysis and need to source raw LC-MS/MS data files (in mzML or NetCDF format) that have been vetted for quality and are known to support FBMN and statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-handling-massive) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-dataset-loading-from-public-repositories**: Use when the analysis target is a metabolomics dataset identified by a MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench accession and the raw processed matrix must be pulled from the repository and converted into a SummarizedExperiment for downstream maplet pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-loading-from-public-repositories))
- **metabolomics-dataset-preprocessing**: Use when combining metabolomics results from multiple studies reported only as aggregate statistics (p-value, fold-change), where compound name synonyms across studies must be resolved before meta-analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-preprocessing))
- **metabolomics-dataset-qc**: Use when validating that a containerized LC-MS metabolomics pipeline (e.g., MS-DIAL -&gt; MS-FLO under Nextflow) produces deterministic, identical results across repeated executions before trusting downstream analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dataset-qc))
- **metabolomics-de**: Load when running two-group metabolomics DE (t-test + log2FC + BH-FDR + PCA) on a feature × sample CSV using `--group-a-prefix` / `--group-b-prefix` (default `ctrl` / `treat`). Skip when needing tunable test backends (use metabolomics-statistics); raw spectra. ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-de) · ★161 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-de**: Load when running two-group metabolomics DE (t-test + log2FC + BH-FDR + PCA) on a feature × sample CSV using `--group-a-prefix` / `--group-b-prefix` (default `ctrl` / `treat`). Skip when needing tunable test backends (use `metabolomics-statistics` for Wilcoxon / ANOVA / Kruskal) or for raw spectra. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/External_Collections/OmicsClaw/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-de) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-de**: Metabolomics differential analysis using univariate tests (t-test, FDR), multivariate methods (PCA, PLS-DA, OPLS-DA, sPLS-DA), Random Forest, and ROC analysis for biomarker discovery. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-de) · ★36)

- **metabolomics-diagnostic-performance-reproduction**: Use when cross-validated supervised model scores (e.g. OPLS-DA scores) are available for pairwise sample groups and you must quantify diagnostic performance as ROC AUC with bootstrap confidence intervals, reproducing reported classifier values. - **metabolomics-dimension-transformation**: Use when a raw metabolite abundance matrix (e.g. plasma_breast_cancer.csv with metabolite names as features) must be converted into a per-sample pathway dysregulation score (PDS) matrix before feature selection or classification in Lilikoi. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dimension-transformation))
- **metabolomics-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a normalized, binned NMR spectra matrix (0.1 ppm rectangular bins, TotalArea-normalized) and need an unsupervised variance decomposition or a scree/cumulative-explained-variance summary before downstream classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-dimensionality-reduction))
- **metabolomics-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a metabolite-by-isolate abundance matrix (hundreds to thousands of features) and need to test whether samples cluster by a known categorical label, such as starter-culture taxonomy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/metabolomics-dimensionality-reduction))
- **metabolomics-direct-infusion-router**: Use for any metabolomics — direct-infusion-MS how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/packs/metabolomics/direct-infusion/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-drift-correction**: Use when a metabolomics or proteomics feature-intensity table lacks real QC samples and signal drift correction/normalization must be driven by simulated QC data, or when benchmarking linear vs nonlinear regression for correction. - **metabolomics-drift-normalization**: Use when a metabolomics intensity matrix (rawdata: compounds × sample runs) shows drift/batch effects across runs with QC samples interleaved, and drift correction is needed before differential abundance testing. - **metabolomics-drift-simulation**: Use when you need ground-truth verification of normalization performance (QC-RLSC, QC-RSC, rLOESS, rGAM, tGAM) under varying outlier counts (0/1/2/4) and QC densities (1 or 2 QC per 10 biological samples), avoiding QC-RSD-style benchmarking pitfalls. - **metabolomics-eic-extraction**: Use when raw LC/MS mzML files and a detected peak list (m/z, RT columns, e.g. from XCMS) must be converted into aligned EIC matrices before peak classification or curation. - **metabolomics-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a metabolite table (names/concentrations plus metadata) from a comparative metabolomics experiment and need enrichment of metabolite classes and KEGG pathways for a species of choice. - **metabolomics-entry-retrieval-via-rest-api**: Use when a task requires fetching a BMRB metabolomics (small molecule) entry — e.g. its assigned chemical shifts or spectral data — programmatically from api.bmrb.io rather than browsing the BMRB website or FTP server. - **metabolomics-er-status-classification**: Use when you have a labeled metabolomics matrix (e.g., 271 breast cancer samples × 162 metabolites with ER+/ER− labels) and need supervised classification plus model comparison. - **metabolomics-experiment-metadata-setup**: Use when starting an eRah GC-MS untargeted metabolomics analysis: you have raw chromatogram files (e.g. NetCDF/CDF from gcspikelite) plus sample class/group labels and need a MetaboSet experiment object before calling setDecPar or deconvolveComp. - **metabolomics-experiment-object-handling**: Use when when converting raw metabolomics data from external formats (tab-delimited text, Sciex OS exports) into a unified R analysis environment, or when you have an existing SummarizedExperiment from another pipeline (e. - **metabolomics-exploratory-data-analysis**: Use when a metabolomics matrix plus sample metadata table (e.g. plasma_breast_cancer.csv with its demo metadata) needs dimension-reduction visualization or assessment of demographic confounder effects before classification. - **metabolomics-feature-annotation**: Use when an LC-MS peak/feature table contains unannotated features suspected of forming homologue series (repeating mass differences with a shared RT trend) and you need series identifiers attached per feature. - **metabolomics-feature-annotation**: Use when rerunning khipu annotation on a preprocessed feature table (e.g. from asari) and the default isotope/adduct patterns leave many features unexplained, so experiment-specific adducts must be supplied to extend coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-annotation--ff44f039a3fb02dc))
- **metabolomics-feature-annotation-against-msms-library**: Use when a feature table or MGF of MS/MS spectra from an untargeted metabolomics experiment needs compound-level annotations against a reference library (e.g., GNPS Drug Library) to enable drug-exposure readouts or lipid-class testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-annotation-against-msms-library))
- **metabolomics-feature-autoscaling**: Use when a preprocessed LC-MS metabolomics feature table (post drift correction, flagging and imputation) must be normalized and scaled before multivariate statistical models in the notame workflow. - **metabolomics-feature-classification-inference**: Use when a pre-trained peak-quality classifier must be applied to an independent XCMS-derived test set from the same analytical platform to flag low-quality peak integrations and score classifier performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-classification-inference))
- **metabolomics-feature-correspondence-mapping**: Use when you need to cross-validate an untargeted metabolomics quantification pipeline (e.g., eRah deconvolution areas) against an alternative peak-picking feature table (e. - **metabolomics-feature-count-validation**: Use when you have an MZmine-exported full-scan feature table (.csv with row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak area) and must verify that a regression-based feature filter reproduces published filtered-feature counts and MS1 recovery percentages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-count-validation))
- **metabolomics-feature-cutoff-sensitivity-sweep**: Use when an LC/MS metabolomics experiment produces a significant feature list chosen by a univariate cutoff and you need to check whether mummichog's predicted metabolite network is stable to that cutoff choice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-cutoff-sensitivity-sweep))
- **metabolomics-feature-cv-assessment**: Use when you have a feature intensity matrix from untargeted metabolomics (samples in rows, compounds in columns) and a sample legend identifying QC samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-cv-assessment))

- **metabolomics-feature-detection-and-alignment**: Use when you have raw metabolomics mass spectrometry data in mzML or mzXML format and need to extract, align, and normalize metabolic features across multiple samples or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-detection-and-alignment))
- **metabolomics-feature-differential-analysis**: Use when a UPLC-HRMS metabolomics feature table (samples × m/z features) must be tested per feature for a two-group diet/treatment effect and the candidate list of significantly altered metabolites (mostly lipids) must be reported with a volcano plot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-differential-analysis))
- **metabolomics-feature-extraction-and-export**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data (mzXML format or pre-computed feature tables from external software) and need to: (1) detect both Gaussian and non-Gaussian shaped metabolic features across multiple samples, (2) align these features across samples, and (3) export EIC chromatograms with m/z.
