- **metabolomics-feature-extraction-mzmine**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS files (e.g., MassIVE accession MSV000080673) must be converted into a per-sample feature table with extracted MS/MS spectra before spectral library matching of drugs or metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-extraction-mzmine))
- **metabolomics-feature-filtering-and-imputation**: Use when you have a raw feature table from LC-MS or DIMS peak detection/alignment (e.g. XCMS output) that must be cleaned of low-quality, blank-derived or non-reproducible peaks and made statistics-ready via imputation and normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-filtering-and-imputation))
- **metabolomics-feature-group-tabulation**: Use when a khipu annotation (JSON empirical-compound trees) of LC-MS isotope-tracing data must be parsed into per-compound isotope-index distributions for labeled vs unlabeled sample groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-group-tabulation))
- **metabolomics-feature-importance-ranking**: Use when replicating a published disease-severity classifier (e.g. recovered vs deceased COVID-19 outcomes from longitudinal metabolomics data) and you must check that model performance and the ranked important metabolite features match the original study. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-importance-ranking))
- **metabolomics-feature-integration-assessment**: Use when after XCMS peak picking and fillPeaks() when you have xcmsEIC and filled xcmsSet objects and need to systematically flag low-quality or unreliable peak integrations prior to statistical modeling or machine learning classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-integration-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-intensity-normalization**: Use when after peak detection and feature table construction (rows = features, columns = samples with intensity values) and before applying intensity-based filters (e.g., fold-change, phenotype score) or when preparing data for dashboard visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-intensity-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-intensity-table-processing**: Use when a metabolomics feature intensity table contains blank injections and features flagged as 'potential contaminants' (mean in real samples not exceeding three times the blank mean), and you must decide which contaminant peaks to discard before data analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-intensity-table-processing))
- **metabolomics-feature-matrix-filtering**: Use when you have an aligned MemoMatrix (sample-by-feature occurrence matrix where features are MS2 peaks and neutral losses) and need to remove background noise before applying visualization or clustering techniques (MDS/PCoA, TMAP, Heatmap). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-matrix-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-matrix-pca-analysis**: Use when you already have a grouped metabolomics feature matrix (e.g. from speaq 2.0 peak detection on 1D NMR spectra) and want an unsupervised check that samples separate by a known class factor such as wine color. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-matrix-pca-analysis))
- **metabolomics-feature-matrix-processing**: Use when you have a raw metabolomics intensity matrix with missing or zero values across samples in different experimental groups, and you need to input it into pathway activity scoring methods (PLAGE, ORA, GSEA) or metabolite set analysis pipelines that require normalized, zero-mean unit-variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-matrix-processing))
- **metabolomics-feature-metadata-joining**: Use when upstream msFeaST pipeline tables (embedding coordinates, cluster labels, globaltest statistics, contrast mappings) must be joined and serialized into a dashboard-ready msFeaST results .json file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-metadata-joining))
- **metabolomics-feature-network-analysis**: Use when you have a list of statistically significant LC-MS features (m/z, retention time) selected by MB-VIP permutation testing (p &lt; 0.01) and need to group them into isotopologue, adduct, cross-assay and correlation clusters to prioritize cross-assay biomarkers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-network-analysis))
- **metabolomics-feature-normalization-scaling**: Use when a preprocessed metabolomics or proteomics feature table (after missing-value imputation) still contains features on incomparable numeric scales and the goal is normalized data for downstream EDA or statistical analysis in POMAShiny/POMA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-normalization-scaling))
- **metabolomics-feature-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw profile LC-MS data in .mzML format and need to prepare regions of interest (ROI) as input for a CNN-Transformer peak detection network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-preprocessing) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-quality-assessment**: Use when after drift correction and before missing value imputation when your LC-MS peak table contains features with variable detection rates across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-quality-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-quality-control**: Use when starting from a raw LC/MS feature table (m/z, retention time, intensity per sample class) that must be QC-filtered before pathway or network activity analysis such as mummichog. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-quality-control))
- **metabolomics-feature-quality-filtering**: Use when a feature-by-sample metabolomics intensity table must be filtered to retain only high-quality features before computing normalization factors, and per-feature failure reasons are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-quality-filtering))
- **metabolomics-feature-requantification**: Use when an untargeted LC-MS/MS feature table from OpenMS pre-processing contains missing values across samples and a complete quantification matrix is needed for statistical analysis, marker selection, or benchmarking detection/quantification rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-requantification))
- **metabolomics-feature-scaling**: Use when a normalized metabolomics feature table (mass features x samples) must be brought onto a comparable scale prior to statistical analysis or visualization, and a choice among centering/scaling variants is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-scaling))
- **metabolomics-feature-scoring**: Use when you have an XCMS-generated peak list (mz/rt columns) from LC/MS metabolomics data plus raw mzML files, and need to separate true peaks from artifacts like noise or co-eluting degeneracies before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-scoring))
- **metabolomics-feature-scoring-and-basketing**: Use when untargeted metabolomics peak lists (mzML, MZmine 2, or GNPS outputs) must be linked to bioassay data to identify bioactive MS features in extract or prefraction libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-scoring-and-basketing))
- **metabolomics-feature-selection-significance-filtering**: Use when you have fitted an MB-PLS model on multi-assay LC-MS intensity data (e.g., HPOS, LPOS, LNEG), computed MB-VIP scores for all features, and need to identify which features are statistically significant for your phenotypic outcome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-selection-significance-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-statistical-testing**: Use when you have an aligned, quantified feature table (uncorrected or batch-corrected apLCMS/XCMS/two-stage output) and two labeled sample groups (e.g. oak vs wine yeast cell types) and need to count significant features at a stated FDR cutoff such as 0.2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-statistical-testing))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-clustering**: Use when a wide formatted metabolomics feature table (samples as columns, features as rows) plus a companion design file need unsupervised hierarchical clustering with dendrograms and pairwise distance output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-clustering))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-comparison**: Use when two untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature tables (samples x features with RT, m/z, and optionally FI medians) must be matched, and match counts/quality should be compared against metabCombiner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-comparison))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-construction**: Use when raw mzML files from multiple public LC/MS untargeted metabolomics studies (HILIC positive mode, serum) must be turned into a single parameter-free, drift-corrected feature matrix of robust peak groups for downstream modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-construction--02e14c070cd310ac))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-construction**: Use when converting eRah deconvolved GC/MS (e.g. GC–TOF EI) chromatograms across multiple samples into a compound-by-sample area table, and compounds must be grouped across samples (alignment) and missing entries recovered before downstream quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-construction--28593578ea24e31c))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-conversion**: Use when you have generated a feature table via mzrtsim() with simulated LC/GC-MS abundances, condition assignments, and batch labels, and you need to pass it to Bioconductor tools (e.g., for batch correction, normalization, or statistical analysis) that expect SummarizedExperiment-class input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-table-curation**: Use when you have a raw feature table (TSV/CSV) derived from LC-MS peak detection (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-curation) · 4 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-table-differential-abundance-testing**: Use when a negative-ion-mode LC-MS feature table with grouped samples (e.g. 94 samples across 14 lamprey tissues) must be tested for features selectively enriched in one group versus all others at a controlled FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-differential-abundance-testing))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-filtering**: Use when when you have a raw LC-MS peak table imported from vendor software (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-filtering) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-feature-table-generation**: Use when the input is raw LC-MS/MS spectral files (e.g. mzML/mzXML from untargeted metabolomics, including pooled-QC SWATH-DIA acquisitions) and the goal is a feature_table.csv of MS1 peak features with compound annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-generation--f513762b0eaf782d))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-generation**: Use when raw centroid-mode LC–MS mzML files must be converted into a per-sample feature matrix with (Rt, m/z) descriptors for downstream QC filters or statistical analysis in untargeted metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-generation--828a498b7ff15aac))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-matching**: Use when you have a RegFilter-exported scheduled precursor list (.csv) and MZmine MS1 feature tables and need to compute per-strain overlap percentages and common-feature counts between SPL and DDA precursors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-matching))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-mgf-preparation**: Use when MZmine-preprocessed full-scan and MS/MS data (e.g. from RegFilter-scheduled precursor lists) must be exported as a feature table (.csv) and spectra (.mgf) for FBMN-based annotation of ions missed by DDA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-mgf-preparation))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-parsing**: Use when raw preprocessing tool exports (XCMS, MS-DIAL, MZmine, Compound Discoverer, Progenesis QI) or csv/Excel feature tables must be parsed and normalized into a feature-table-plus-metadata structure before metabolomics statistical analysis or reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-parsing))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-processing**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS outputs (feature/quant tables plus MGF MS/MS spectra) must be turned into a chemical hierarchy for sample-level chemical comparison — e.g., weighted UniFrac on Qemistree trees vs Bray-Curtis on food metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-processing))
- **metabolomics-feature-table-wrangling**: Use when a pcpfm/Asari feature table (preferred_drop_missing.tsv or full_feature_table.tsv) still contains missing values, infrequent features, or failed-injection samples, and you need a curated, ready-for-statistics table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-feature-table-wrangling))
- **metabolomics-feature-transformation**: Use when when you have a feature intensity table (samples × compounds) from targeted or non-targeted metabolomics and need to prepare it for statistical modeling or multivariate analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-feature-transformation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-figure-s10-reproduction**: Use when evaluating whether artifact-aware peak reintegration improves LC/MS peak boundary definition — i.e., when comparing pre- vs post-reintegration peak-area CV distributions for feature lists scored by a trained PeakDetective classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-figure-s10-reproduction))
- **metabolomics-file-format-parsing**: Use when you have mwTab-formatted files from the Metabolomics Workbench containing MS or NMR experimental metadata and tabular data sections (e.g., METABOLITES, DATA blocks), and need to load them into memory for downstream conversion, validation, or analysis rather than manual text parsing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-file-format-parsing))
- **metabolomics-file-schedule-monitoring**: Use when a QC4Metabolomics deployment (Docker container with continuous processing and module_File_schedule enabled) must be validated for near-real-time detection of newly acquired mzML/mzXML/mzData files, or when reporting maximum detection latency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-file-schedule-monitoring))
- **metabolomics-filename-sanitization-for-workflow-inputs**: Use when preparing proprietary vendor RAW LC-MS files for Nextflow4MS-DIAL ingestion, before placing converted .mzML or .abf files into data/raw_data/ for processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-filename-sanitization-for-workflow-inputs))
- **metabolomics-functional-annotation**: Use when a metabolomics study has a list of identified metabolites (e.g. HMDB or KEGG compound IDs) and the goal is to map them to KEGG pathways, rank pathways by metabolite occurrence, and produce annotated pathway-map links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-functional-annotation))
- **metabolomics-functional-prediction-workflow-validation**: Use when a Python-based metabolomics analysis package has been relocated to a new GitHub organization (e.g., metabolomics-cloud) and you need to confirm that the migration preserved package integrity, installation, and runtime correctness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-functional-prediction-workflow-validation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-gc-ms-router**: Use for any metabolomics — GC-MS how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. Searches 367 evidence-grounded metabolomics — GC-MS skills distilled from method papers and their code, then reads the one that fits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/packs/metabolomics/gc-ms/skills/_router))
- **metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics**: Use when you have a metabolomics matrix with metabolites as rows and samples as columns, each sample labeled with one of two experimental group identifiers (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics))
- **metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics**: Use when a preprocessed metabolomics concentration table (CSV/XLSX, samples as rows, metabolite columns, a group/factor column) has two or more sample groups and you need per-metabolite significance plus pairwise inter-group p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics--7b31d1b5e1803e9b))
- **metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics**: Use when the input is a metabolite-by-sample count data frame with sample metadata on one or two grouping factors and the goal is per-metabolite fold change, adjusted p values, and group-level visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-group-comparison-statistics--6c1d5958b023dcdd))
- **metabolomics-high-dimensional-correlation-analysis**: Use when a samples-by-metabolites high-dimensional feature matrix requires an M-by-M dependency matrix for eigen-analysis-based Meff estimation, and Pearson correlation is suspected of missing non-linear dependence among metabolite features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-high-dimensional-correlation-analysis))
- **metabolomics-html-report-generation**: Use when a metabolomics LC-MS dataset has been preprocessed, statistically analysed and visualised (e.g. the bundled cancerCell demo in MetaboReport) and the results must be delivered as one self-contained HTML document to lab members or service clients. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-html-report-generation))
- **metabolomics-imputation-benchmarking**: Use when targeted metabolomics (FFA/BA LC-MS) matrices contain LOD/LOQ-driven MNAR gaps and you must objectively choose an imputation method rather than substituting a single value blindly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-imputation-benchmarking))
- **metabolomics-imputation-method-application**: Use when your metabolomics dataset (LC/MS or GC/MS) contains missing values encoded as NA or zero that represent compounds below the instrument's limit of detection (LOD) or limit of quantification (LOQ), rather than values missing completely at random. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-imputation-method-application) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-input-table-formatting**: Use when preparing TSV compound-annotation tables as TurboPutative inputs and confirming end-to-end processing time scales acceptably with table size before submitting larger untargeted-metabolomics datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-input-table-formatting))
- **metabolomics-intensity-log-transformation**: Use when raw (untransformed) metabolomics feature intensities (e.g., a compounds x samples matrix such as the 393-run, 269-metabolite ENVIRONAGE urine dataset) feed into Metanorm normalization (tGAM, rGAM, rLOESS, QC-RLSC, QC-RSC) or downstream DAM testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-intensity-log-transformation))
- **metabolomics-intensity-normalization**: Use when raw metabolite-by-sample intensity data (e.g. plasma_breast_cancer.csv) requires normalization or imputation prior to pathway scoring or classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-intensity-normalization))
- **metabolomics-intensity-normalization**: Use when your input is a raw metabolomics intensity matrix (compounds × samples) with known batch assignment and QC sample labels, and you observe signal drift across the analytical sequence or batch-to-batch variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-intensity-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-intensity-transformation-and-scaling**: Use when raw targeted or untargeted feature tables contain missing intensity values and non-normal, heteroscedastic intensities that must be transformed before running t-tests, fold-change, ANOVA, or regression in GetFeatistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-intensity-transformation-and-scaling))
- **metabolomics-ion-mobility-router**: Use for any metabolomics — ion-mobility-MS how-to question (which method, tool, parameters or pipeline to use), before answering from memory. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/packs/metabolomics/ion-mobility/skills/_router))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-json-data-serialization))
- **metabolomics-language-model-pretraining**: Use when you have large-scale population NMR metabolomics profiles (e.g., UK Biobank plasma NMR panels with ~249 metabolite features) and need a pre-trained foundation model for downstream multi-disease prediction or risk stratification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-language-model-pretraining))
- **metabolomics-lcms-data-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw LC-MS metabolomics data from multiple disease groups (e.g., .mzML or .npy format files) that must be converted into a uniform, normalized feature representation before training a deep learning classifier to distinguish disease states. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-lcms-data-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-library-generation-pipeline**: Use when when you have cloned or accessed the Reverse_metabolomics_library_generation repository and need to verify that the Nextflow workflow is properly configured, executable, and produces expected spectral library outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-library-generation-pipeline))
- **metabolomics-linear-model-statistics**: Use when a metabolomics dataset (e.g. a MetaboLights accession such as MTBLS1) has been loaded and preprocessed in an R/SummarizedExperiment container and per-metabolite association testing (linear models, statistical analysis) is the next analytical step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-linear-model-statistics))
- **metabolomics-lipidomics**: Specialized lipidomics analysis for lipid identification, quantification, and pathway interpretation. Covers LC-MS lipidomics with LipidSearch, MS-DIAL, and LipidMaps annotation. Use when analyzing lipid classes, chain composition, or lipid-specific pathways. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-lipidomics) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-log-transformation**: Use when a plasma/serum NMR metabolomics matrix contains zero values (below detection limit) that must be shifted, log-transformed, and z-scaled per cohort before survival or association modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-log-transformation))
- **metabolomics-log-transformation-scaling**: Use when preparing targeted LC/MS or GC/MS metabolomics matrices (e.g., real_data.csv, targeted_data.csv) with non-normal variable distributions and missing values, before running GS_impute or comparable imputation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-log-transformation-scaling))
- **metabolomics-matrix-csv-io**: Use when a metabolomics analysis step needs its input read from or written to CSV as a metabolite x sample matrix — e.g. before lilikoi.preproc_norm/preproc_knn or after producing normalized/imputed tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-matrix-csv-io))
- **metabolomics-matrix-manipulation**: Use when you have a raw metabolomics abundance table (e.g., LC/MS or GC/MS peak intensities or concentrations) with non-normal distributions and missing values, and you need to prepare it for Gibbs sampler or other model-based imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-matrix-manipulation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-matrix-manipulation-r**: Use when you have a log2-scaled metabolomics feature matrix in CSV format with samples in rows, metabolic features in columns, and batch identifiers in the first column, and you need to remove technical heterogeneity or drift across analytical batches before estimating biological mechanisms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-matrix-manipulation-r))
- **metabolomics-matrix-normalization**: Use when a log-transformed, missing-value-imputed metabolomics peak-intensity matrix (featuredata format) needs sample-wise scaling normalization, or when comparing sum/median/mean/reference scaling variants before downstream differential or classification analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-matrix-normalization))
- **metabolomics-meta-analysis-pvalue-aggregation**: Use when metabolomics studies report only overall statistical parameters (P-value, fold-change, study size n) without raw data or standard deviations, and per-compound combined P-values are needed for meta-analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-meta-analysis-pvalue-aggregation))
- **metabolomics-metabolite-annotation**: Metabolite identification from m/z and retention time. Covers database matching, MS/MS spectral matching, and confidence level assignment. Use when assigning compound identities to detected features in untargeted metabolomics. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-metabolite-annotation) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-metadata-curation**: Use when preparing spectral metadata records (e.g. LC-MS/MS spectra from a urine analytical matrix) for import into PeakForest, where users must supply completely-cleaned data from an Excel-like template. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-metadata-curation))
- **metabolomics-metadata-parsing**: Use when raw inputs are a metabolomics study's processed data matrix plus repository metadata (MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench accession) that must be converted into a SummarizedExperiment before running maplet pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-metadata-parsing))
- **metabolomics-metadata-quality-checking**: Use when you have downloaded JSON project documents from the PoDP Zenodo archive and need to verify, per project, that each document conforms to the community-defined project format before downstream data mining or linking analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-metadata-quality-checking))
- **metabolomics-method-benchmarking**: Use when a new feature-correspondence method must be compared against a published alternative (e.g. metabCombiner) by running both on the same reference/target feature-table pairs and tabulating match counts and quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-method-benchmarking))
- **metabolomics-missing-data-handling**: Use when a metabolomics or lipidomics feature table contains widespread missing intensities and the analysis must decide, per feature, whether gaps reflect measurement dropout to impute or genuine absence to zero-fill — e.g. heterogeneous datasets mixing endogenous metabolites and xenobiotics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-data-handling))
- **metabolomics-missing-data-preprocessing**: Use when a targeted metabolomics data matrix (e.g., LC/MS FFA/BA data) contains left-censored MNAR missing values caused by LOD/LOQ and a complete matrix is needed before PLS-DA or other analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-data-preprocessing))
- **metabolomics-missing-feature-subsampling**: Use when evaluating the stability of a pathway ranking method on metabolomics peak data where missing peaks and noise are prevalent, and you need a quantitative robustness comparison against alternative methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-feature-subsampling))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-and-outlier-exclusion**: Use when a targeted LC-MS metabolomics dataset (tab-delimited peak areas with aliquot, compound, area, type, injection_time, batch columns) contains NA values or samples flagged as statistical outliers in QC samples, and downstream calculations must exclude them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-and-outlier-exclusion))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-filtering**: Use when preprocessing heterogeneous metabolomics/xenobiotics feature-intensity tables where some variables show high missingness and you must decide per variable whether missingness means absence (zero) or data loss, and filter variables excessively missing across every study group. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-filtering))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-filtering-and-imputation**: Use when raw metabolomics peak-intensity tables (e.g., TEDDY ST001386/ST001636 from Metabolomics Workbench) contain missing values and must be made complete before building DNEA partial correlation networks or comparing against marginal-correlation tools like MetaBox2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-filtering-and-imputation))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a targeted LC/MS metabolomics data matrix contains left-censored missing values caused by LOD/LOQ (MNAR) and imputation quality must be evaluated before univariate or multivariate analysis such as PLS-DA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-imputation--c1595b46a37b0721))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a metabolite abundance matrix has missing values generated by MNAR (metabolite below the LOD) combined with MAR dropout, and imputation quality must preserve both RMSE accuracy and downstream significance-testing metabolite lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-imputation--e9294401416ad65c))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a metabolomics feature table (samples as columns, features as rows) contains missing values that must be filled before downstream statistics such as ANOVA, PLS-DA, or random forest classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-imputation--2776e250e5a7b1e2))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-imputation**: Use when raw metabolomics data matrices contain zero values or NA entries representing undetected metabolic features. Imputation is required before batch effect correction models (ComBat, ber) can be reliably applied, as these models assume complete feature matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-imputation))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-masking**: Use when a feature count matrix destined for RALPS normalization contains missing values, or when stress-testing normalization robustness by randomly replacing matrix values with the 1000-count minimum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-masking))
- **metabolomics-missing-value-qc-and-imputation**: Use when a Nightingale 1H-NMR metabolomics matrix (CSV/TSV) contains missing or zero metabolite values that must be QC-filtered and imputed before computing metaboAge, surrogate, or published clinical scores in MiMIR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missing-value-qc-and-imputation))
- **metabolomics-missingness-simulation**: Use when benchmarking metabolite imputation algorithms on real metabolomics data where missingness combines MNAR (below limit of detection) and MAR mechanisms, and averaged RMSE comparisons across missingness levels are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-missingness-simulation))
- **metabolomics-model-coefficient-application**: Use when you have a matrix of Nightingale Health 1H-NMR metabolomics measurements (samples × features) and need to generate predicted metabolic scores published in peer-reviewed studies (MetaboAge, mortality score, cardiovascular event risk, Type-2 diabetes score, COVID-severity score, or surrogate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-model-coefficient-application))
- **metabolomics-model-performance-comparison**: Use when you have trained multiple machine learning classifiers (e.g., AdaBoost, SVM, Random Forest) on the same metabolomics peak-quality training set using k-fold cross-validation with repeated runs (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-model-performance-comparison) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-ms2-spectral-matching**: Use when annotating LC-MS metabolite features from a tidyMass mass_dataset object against a reference library, and you must assign MSI annotation levels from m/z, RT, and MS2 evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-ms2-spectral-matching))
- **metabolomics-msdial-preprocessing**: MS-DIAL-based metabolomics preprocessing as alternative to XCMS. Covers peak detection, alignment, annotation, and export for downstream analysis. Use when processing MS-DIAL output files for R/Python analysis or when preferring GUI-based preprocessing. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-msdial-preprocessing) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-multi-batch-normalization**: Use when a feature-count matrix (e.g. 170 metabolites x 2856 samples) spans multiple LC-MS batches with reference samples (pooled/NIST 1950 SRM) present in each batch and interbatch differences must be removed before analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-multi-batch-normalization))
- **metabolomics-multiple-testing-correction**: Use when a metabolomics pipeline needs to control false discoveries: selecting differentially abundant metabolites from two-tailed t-tests, or converting raw ORA pathway p-values into q-values before declaring significant enriched pathways (SEPs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-multiple-testing-correction))
- **metabolomics-multivariate-score-extraction**: Use when a preprocessed metabolomics/proteomics feature table (samples x metabolites, e.g. from ST000284-style targeted studies) must be reduced or classified, and per-sample PCA/PLS-DA scores plus a discriminative metabolite list are the required deliverables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-multivariate-score-extraction))
- **metabolomics-na-imputation**: Use when a tab-delimited feature abundance table (e.g. from MS-DIAL, such as margheRita's 'Urine' dataset) contains NA metabolite abundances that must be filled with low, plausible values before downstream statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-na-imputation))
- **metabolomics-network-construction**: Use when you have a normalized metabolite concentration table (e.g. ~90 small molecules quantified by 1H-NMR across cell populations) and need a sparse network of conditional dependencies among metabolite features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-network-construction))
- **metabolomics-network-stability-selection**: Use when building metabolite-metabolite networks from two-condition metabolomics data with p &gt;&gt; n, after BIC-tuning lambda, and you need per-edge selection probabilities to reweight regularization before getNetworks(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-network-stability-selection))
- **metabolomics-network-visualization**: Use when you have MetDNA2/KGMN pipeline output (e.g. the Demo_MetDNA2_NIST_urine_pos/06_visualization/ folder) and need to reconstruct, inspect, or render multi-layer metabolite networks or reproduce published annotation benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-network-visualization))
- **metabolomics-nmr-data-processing**: Use when a series of 1D-NMR spectra must be phase-corrected and pre-processed (aligned, scaled, variance-stabilised) before multivariate statistical analysis, and a phase-corrected reference spectrum is available to seed ph0/ph1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-nmr-data-processing))
- **metabolomics-nmr-spectra-quality-control**: Use when a series of 1D-NMR spectra (e.g. Daphnia magna extracts) must be prepared for multi-variate statistical analysis and peaks drift between spectra, requiring alignment QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-nmr-spectra-quality-control))
- **metabolomics-noise-perturbation-simulation**: Use when when benchmarking or validating a pathway analysis method (such as PALS, ORA, or GSEA) on metabolomics data, you need quantitative evidence that the method's pathway rankings remain stable despite noise and missing peaks—conditions prevalent in real LC-MS/MS datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-noise-perturbation-simulation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolomics-normalization**: Load when normalising a feature × sample metabolomics CSV via median, quantile, total (sum), PQN (probabilistic quotient), or log methods — emits a normalised wide-form table. Skip when also imputing (use metabolomics-quantification); raw spectra (use metabolomics-xcms-preprocessing). ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-normalization) · ★161 · 2 copies)

- **metabolomics-normalization**: Metabolomics data normalization, scaling and transformation. - **metabolomics-normalization**: Load when normalising a feature × sample metabolomics CSV via median, quantile, total (sum), PQN (probabilistic quotient), or log methods — emits a normalised wide-form table. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/External_Collections/OmicsClaw/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-normalization) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-normalization-artifact-reproduction**: Use when you have raw metabolomics intensity data (rows = compounds, columns = samples) with batch annotations and QC/biological sample labels, and you need to select among five normalization methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-normalization-artifact-reproduction))
- **metabolomics-normalization-benchmark-execution**: Use when comparing computational throughput of metabolomics normalization methods across parallel core counts, or when estimating how long tGAM/rGAM/rLOESS/TIGER/SERFF will take on a targeted or untargeted dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-normalization-benchmark-execution))
- **metabolomics-normalization-pipeline-comparison**: Use when replicate-containing metabolomics data have missing values and you must choose between imputation methods (BPCA, kNN, RF-median, RF-quantile) crossed with normalizations (quantile, median, RUV), scored by reproducibility rather than CV/RSD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-normalization-pipeline-comparison))
- **metabolomics-normalization-qc**: Quality control and normalization for metabolomics data. Covers QC-based correction, batch effect removal, and data transformation methods. Use when correcting technical variation in metabolomics data before statistical analysis. ([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/metabolomics-normalization-qc) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-npp-reliability-assessment**: Use when you have completed NPP runs from one or more metabolomics tools on a set of LC-HRMS mzML files AND you have reference information (target molecule list with molecular formula, main adduct, and RT boundaries) available for a subset of expected compounds in those files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-npp-reliability-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-ora-methodology**: Use when you have a metabolomics dataset and want to perform pathway enrichment analysis using ORA, but need to first understand its behavior, limitations, and correct application through reproducible simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-ora-methodology) · 2 copies)
- **metabolomics-over-representation-analysis**: Use when you have a list of differentially abundant metabolites (e.g., two-tailed t-test with BH FDR q ≤ 0.05) plus an assay-specific background set, and need pathway-level functional interpretation with p-value sensitivity checks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-over-representation-analysis))
- **metabolomics-overall-results-data-handling**: Use when input is a compound-level table from metabolomics studies where only the compound identifier, trend/behaviour label (up-regulated, down-regulated, or no trend) and bibliographic reference are disclosed, and a per-compound qualitative meta-analysis vote count is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-overall-results-data-handling))
- **metabolomics-overall-results-meta-analysis**: Use when combining per-compound results from multiple metabolomics studies that report only P-value and fold-change (no SD/variance), needing a weighted combined significance and effect size per compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-overall-results-meta-analysis))
- **metabolomics-parameter-extraction**: Use when you have raw untargeted metabolomics data in mzML, mzXML, or CDF format from qTOF, Orbitrap, or FTICR mass analyzers, at least 3 samples, a sample metadata spreadsheet linking filenames to experimental factors, and need to generate optimized processing parameters for XCMS or MZmine2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolomics-parameter-extraction))
- **metabolomics-parameter-optimization**: Use when starting untargeted metabolomics analysis from raw instrument files (.mzML, .mzXML, .CDF) and no dataset-specific centWave/peak-picking parameters exist yet; manual tuning is impractical or a universal parameter set is unavailable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-parameter-optimization))
- **metabolomics-parameter-translation**: Use when Paramounter has measured universal parameters from raw LC-MS metabolomics data and you need to map them to software-specific parameters (e.g., mzdiff for XCMS CentWave) before feature extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-parameter-translation))
- **metabolomics-parameter-trend-analysis**: Use when comparing metabolomics feature lists acquired under different MS parameter settings (collision energy, ion energy, capillary voltage) to quantify ISF vs. real metabolic feature trends. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolomics-parameter-trend-analysis))
- **metabolomics-pathway-enrichment**: Load when running metabolite-name ORA against an explicit local pathway reference with BH-FDR; bundled pathway sets are for explicit demonstrations only. Skip m/z annotation (use metabolomics-annotation) and topology analysis or online pathway retrieval (use external mummichog / FELLA or database tools). ([TianGzlab/OmicsClaw](https://github.com/TianGzlab/OmicsClaw/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-pathway-enrichment) · ★161)
- **metabolomics-pathway-enrichment**: Metabolomics pathway analysis using MetaboAnalystR (KEGG, Reactome), pathview visualization, MSEA, mummichog, and network-based topology analysis. ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-pathway-enrichment) · ★36)
- **metabolomics-pathway-enrichment**: Load when running over-representation analysis (ORA) on a metabolite list via Fisher's exact test against a built-in 9-pathway DEMO dictionary, BH-FDR adjusted. Skip when needing real KEGG / Reactome (this skill is demo-only) or `mummichog` / `fella` topology methods (CLI accepts them but only ORA runs). ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/External_Collections/OmicsClaw/skills/metabolomics/metabolomics-pathway-enrichment) · ★36 · 2 copies)

- **metabolomics-pathway-enrichment**: Load when running over-representation analysis (ORA) on a metabolite list via Fisher's exact test against a built-in 9-pathway DEMO dictionary, BH-FDR adjusted. - **metabolomics-pathway-enrichment-execution**: Use when a metabolomics study yields a list of significant metabolites (KEGG IDs or names) and the goal is fast, local pathway enrichment and network analysis, especially when speed or reproducibility matters. - **metabolomics-pathway-mapping**: Map metabolites to biological pathways using KEGG, Reactome, and MetaboAnalyst. Perform pathway enrichment and topology analysis. - **metabolomics-pca-decomposition**: Use when you have a wide-format metabolomics feature table (samples as columns, features as rows with unique identifiers) plus a design file grouping samples, and you want unsupervised dimension reduction and visualization of sample structure. - **metabolomics-qc-workflow-design**: Use when you must evaluate or correct time-related signal drift in untargeted LC–MS batches that include pooled/intrastudy QC samples acquired in order (e.g., mzML runs from MetaboLights MTBLS1919). - **metabolomics-qcpool-series-monitoring**: Use when a targeted metabolomics or lipidomics study has pooled QC samples (QCpool) measured at regular intervals across one or more sequences, and you need to detect signal drift, background/carryover effects or out-of-control injections before downstream missing-value handling and outlier removal. - **metabolomics-quality-control-monitoring**: Use when untargeted MS-based metabolomics files (mzML, mzXML, mzData) are being acquired or already acquired and you need to monitor QC parameters such as m/z deviation, retention time deviation, intensity drift, calibration offset, or contaminant levels in (near) real time or retrospectively. - **metabolomics-quality-control-report-generation**: Use when after completing outlier detection, batch correction, and quality metric calculation on a SummarizedExperiment object using mzQuality's doAnalysis function, and after manually or automatically filtering compounds and samples using the 'use' column in rowData and colData.
